- **gate-and-ship**: Quality gates then ship — chains `/prepare-delivery` and `/ship` in one command. Runs the full pre-ship pipeline (deslop, simplify, agnix, enhance, multi-agent review, delivery validation, docs sync) and only proceeds to commit/push/PR/merge/deploy if every gate passes. If a gate fails, returns to the implementation phase with concrete fix instructions instead of shipping broken code. Designed for the "I'm done; ship it" workflow where you don't want to think about which gates to run — they all run, in the right order, with the right pass criteria. ([agent-sh/gate-and-ship](https://github.com/agent-sh/gate-and-ship))
